2025-11-28T16:22:49.27417529Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.274072Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.071855ms","elapsed_secs":0.001071855},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.275471019Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.275396Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"786.268µs","elapsed_secs":0.000786268},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.275600622Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.275541Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"798.679µs","elapsed_secs":0.000798679},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.276847401Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.276768Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"674.376µs","elapsed_secs":0.000674376},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.277166618Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.277100Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"888.711µs","elapsed_secs":0.000888711},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.278209282Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.278147Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"717.607µs","elapsed_secs":0.000717607},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.279034071Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.278934Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.139516ms","elapsed_secs":0.001139516},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.280511605Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.280435Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"789.858µs","elapsed_secs":0.000789858},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:22:49.383248989Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:22:49.383080Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"882.5µs","elapsed_secs":0.0008825},"target":"sqlx::query"}

SELECT \n                        s.id,\n                        s.is_active,\n                        s.created_at,\n                        s.category,\n                        -- Extraire titre_service (support des deux formats) - optimisé\n                        COALESCE(\n                            s.data->>'titre_service',\n                            s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur',\n                            s.data->>'titre',\n                            'Service sans titre'\n                        ) as titre_service,\n                        -- Extraire description (tronquée à 200 caractères) - optimisé\n                        LEFT(\n                            COALESCE(\n                                s.data->>'description',\n                                s.data->'description'->>'valeur',\n                                ''\n                            ),\n                            200\n                        ) as description_preview,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Extraction produits simplifiée sans LATERAL JOIN\n                        -- Utilise jsonb_path_query_array qui est plus rapide que jsonb_array_elements\n                        COALESCE(\n                            (\n                                SELECT jsonb_agg(\n                                    jsonb_build_object(\n                                        'nom', COALESCE(\n                                            elem->>'nom', \n                                            elem->>'name', \n                                            elem->>'titre', \n                                            ''\n                                        ),\n                                        'prix', elem->>'prix',\n                                        'devise', elem->>'devise',\n                                        'is_active', COALESCE(\n                                            (SELECT is_active FROM products_lifecycle \n                                             WHERE service_id = s.id \n                                             AND product_index = (ordinality - 1) \n                                             LIMIT 1),\n                                            true\n                                        )\n                                    )\n                                    ORDER BY ordinality\n                                )\n                                FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                                    CASE \n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                        ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                    END\n                                ) WITH ORDINALITY AS t(elem, ordinality)\n                            ),\n                            '[]'::jsonb\n                        ) as produits_light,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Compter produits avec jsonb_array_length (très rapide)\n                        COALESCE(\n                            jsonb_array_length(\n                                CASE \n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                    ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                END\n                            )::BIGINT,\n                            0\n                        ) as produits_count,\n                        -- Garder seulement le place_id de Google Places\n                        s.data->'google_place'->>'place_id' as google_place_id\n                    FROM services s\n                    WHERE s.user_id = $1\n                    ORDER BY s.created_at DESC\n                    LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3\n                    \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"3.425629ms","elapsed_secs":0.003425629},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:23:19.275402516Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.275340Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.057264ms","elapsed_secs":0.001057264},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.276629024Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.276563Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"669.095µs","elapsed_secs":0.000669095},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.276865289Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.276808Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"859.7µs","elapsed_secs":0.0008597},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.277943434Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.277866Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"660.755µs","elapsed_secs":0.000660755},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.278552848Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.278482Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.022423ms","elapsed_secs":0.001022423},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.280009922Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.279916Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"750.457µs","elapsed_secs":0.000750457},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.327406702Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.327273Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.866313ms","elapsed_secs":0.001866313},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:19.383128674Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.382786Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"928.802µs","elapsed_secs":0.000928802},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:23:19.486582724Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.486446Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT delivery_id, suggested_status, created_at, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            delivery_id,\n            suggested_status,\n            created_at,\n            auto_confirm_after_seconds\n        FROM delivery_proximity_suggestions\n        WHERE \n            status = 'pending'\n            AND auto_confirm_after_seconds IS NOT NULL\n            AND created_at + (auto_confirm_after_seconds || ' seconds')::interval <= NOW()\n        LIMIT 50\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"760.458µs","elapsed_secs":0.000760458},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:23:19.488264433Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:19.488166Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"970.043µs","elapsed_secs":0.000970043},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:23:38.642034048Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:38.641916Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"655.075µs","elapsed_secs":0.000655075},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:38.643350789Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:38.643270Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"672.255µs","elapsed_secs":0.000672255},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:38.645210151Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:38.644674Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"722.607µs","elapsed_secs":0.000722607},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:38.646277006Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:38.646178Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"748.477µs","elapsed_secs":0.000748477},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:23:49.274156586Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.274088Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.043654ms","elapsed_secs":0.001043654},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.275720232Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.275655Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"862.06µs","elapsed_secs":0.00086206},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.27605942Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.275999Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.577016ms","elapsed_secs":0.001577016},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.277425811Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.277361Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"659.635µs","elapsed_secs":0.000659635},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.278287331Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.278173Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.765011ms","elapsed_secs":0.001765011},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.279175641Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.279105Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"697.786µs","elapsed_secs":0.000697786},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.280208215Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.280116Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.192018ms","elapsed_secs":0.001192018},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:23:49.282315984Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:23:49.282246Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.415663ms","elapsed_secs":0.001415663},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:19.274178445Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.274089Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.085265ms","elapsed_secs":0.001085265},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.275511405Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.275426Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"772.557µs","elapsed_secs":0.000772557},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.275654239Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.275580Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"854.91µs","elapsed_secs":0.00085491},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.276919748Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.276834Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"717.116µs","elapsed_secs":0.000717116},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.277236775Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.277163Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"915.691µs","elapsed_secs":0.000915691},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.278242788Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.278172Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"661.785µs","elapsed_secs":0.000661785},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.278899453Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.278830Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"993.763µs","elapsed_secs":0.000993763},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.281114224Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.281018Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.515965ms","elapsed_secs":0.001515965},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.329647091Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.329453Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"814.199µs","elapsed_secs":0.000814199},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.383305015Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.383188Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"889.12µs","elapsed_secs":0.00088912},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:19.487120504Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.487008Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, service_id, product_index, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            id,\n            service_id,\n            product_index,\n            client_user_id,\n            provider_user_id\n        FROM product_orders\n        WHERE \n            status = 'pending'\n            AND validation_deadline IS NOT NULL\n            AND validation_deadline <= $1\n        LIMIT 50\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"878.64µs","elapsed_secs":0.00087864},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:19.488571937Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:19.488481Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.052584ms","elapsed_secs":0.001052584},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:35.898585917Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:35.898453Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT s.id, s.is_active, s.created_at, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n                    SELECT \n                        s.id,\n                        s.is_active,\n                        s.created_at,\n                        s.category,\n                        -- Extraire titre_service (support des deux formats) - optimisé\n                        COALESCE(\n                            s.data->>'titre_service',\n                            s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur',\n                            s.data->>'titre',\n                            'Service sans titre'\n                        ) as titre_service,\n                        -- Extraire description (tronquée à 200 caractères) - optimisé\n                        LEFT(\n                            COALESCE(\n                                s.data->>'description',\n                                s.data->'description'->>'valeur',\n                                ''\n                            ),\n                            200\n                        ) as description_preview,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Extraction produits simplifiée sans LATERAL JOIN\n                        -- Utilise jsonb_path_query_array qui est plus rapide que jsonb_array_elements\n                        COALESCE(\n                            (\n                                SELECT jsonb_agg(\n                                    jsonb_build_object(\n                                        'nom', COALESCE(\n                                            elem->>'nom', \n                                            elem->>'name', \n                                            elem->>'titre', \n                                            ''\n                                        ),\n                                        'prix', elem->>'prix',\n                                        'devise', elem->>'devise',\n                                        'is_active', COALESCE(\n                                            (SELECT is_active FROM products_lifecycle \n                                             WHERE service_id = s.id \n                                             AND product_index = (ordinality - 1) \n                                             LIMIT 1),\n                                            true\n                                        )\n                                    )\n                                    ORDER BY ordinality\n                                )\n                                FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                                    CASE \n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                        ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                    END\n                                ) WITH ORDINALITY AS t(elem, ordinality)\n                            ),\n                            '[]'::jsonb\n                        ) as produits_light,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Compter produits avec jsonb_array_length (très rapide)\n                        COALESCE(\n                            jsonb_array_length(\n                                CASE \n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                    ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                END\n                            )::BIGINT,\n                            0\n                        ) as produits_count,\n                        -- Garder seulement le place_id de Google Places\n                        s.data->'google_place'->>'place_id' as google_place_id\n                    FROM services s\n                    WHERE s.user_id = $1\n                    ORDER BY s.created_at DESC\n                    LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3\n                    \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"91.678569ms","elapsed_secs":0.091678569},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:39.967880233Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:39.967775Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT s.id, s.is_active, s.created_at, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n                    SELECT \n                        s.id,\n                        s.is_active,\n                        s.created_at,\n                        s.category,\n                        -- Extraire titre_service (support des deux formats) - optimisé\n                        COALESCE(\n                            s.data->>'titre_service',\n                            s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur',\n                            s.data->>'titre',\n                            'Service sans titre'\n                        ) as titre_service,\n                        -- Extraire description (tronquée à 200 caractères) - optimisé\n                        LEFT(\n                            COALESCE(\n                                s.data->>'description',\n                                s.data->'description'->>'valeur',\n                                ''\n                            ),\n                            200\n                        ) as description_preview,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Extraction produits simplifiée sans LATERAL JOIN\n                        -- Utilise jsonb_path_query_array qui est plus rapide que jsonb_array_elements\n                        COALESCE(\n                            (\n                                SELECT jsonb_agg(\n                                    jsonb_build_object(\n                                        'nom', COALESCE(\n                                            elem->>'nom', \n                                            elem->>'name', \n                                            elem->>'titre', \n                                            ''\n                                        ),\n                                        'prix', elem->>'prix',\n                                        'devise', elem->>'devise',\n                                        'is_active', COALESCE(\n                                            (SELECT is_active FROM products_lifecycle \n                                             WHERE service_id = s.id \n                                             AND product_index = (ordinality - 1) \n                                             LIMIT 1),\n                                            true\n                                        )\n                                    )\n                                    ORDER BY ordinality\n                                )\n                                FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                                    CASE \n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                        WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                        THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                        ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                    END\n                                ) WITH ORDINALITY AS t(elem, ordinality)\n                            ),\n                            '[]'::jsonb\n                        ) as produits_light,\n                        -- ✅ OPTIMISATION: Compter produits avec jsonb_array_length (très rapide)\n                        COALESCE(\n                            jsonb_array_length(\n                                CASE \n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                    WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array' \n                                    THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                    ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                                END\n                            )::BIGINT,\n                            0\n                        ) as produits_count,\n                        -- Garder seulement le place_id de Google Places\n                        s.data->'google_place'->>'place_id' as google_place_id\n                    FROM services s\n                    WHERE s.user_id = $1\n                    ORDER BY s.created_at DESC\n                    LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3\n                    \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"1.654299ms","elapsed_secs":0.001654299},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:45.637838387Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:45.637700Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, service_id, product_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            id,\n            service_id,\n            product_id,\n            product_index,\n            type as media_type,\n            path,\n            COALESCE(is_main_image, FALSE) as is_main_image,\n            COALESCE(display_order, 0) as display_order,\n            uploaded_at,\n            ai_description,\n            ai_tags\n        FROM media\n        WHERE service_id = $1 \n        AND product_index = $2\n        ORDER BY \n            COALESCE(is_main_image, FALSE) DESC,  -- Image principale en premier\n            COALESCE(display_order, 0) ASC,        -- Puis par ordre\n            id ASC                                  -- Puis par ID\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.727259ms","elapsed_secs":0.001727259},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:24:49.273733922Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.273585Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"684.976µs","elapsed_secs":0.000684976},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.273778453Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.273744Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status = 'scheduled' AND starts_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"929.221µs","elapsed_secs":0.000929221},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.275150274Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.275058Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"710.836µs","elapsed_secs":0.000710836},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.275230527Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.275174Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"883.28µs","elapsed_secs":0.00088328},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.276389463Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.276321Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"647.355µs","elapsed_secs":0.000647355},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.276555087Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.276493Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"659.125µs","elapsed_secs":0.000659125},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.277769355Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.277689Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"676.396µs","elapsed_secs":0.000676396},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.278331628Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.278265Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.118076ms","elapsed_secs":0.001118076},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.279631868Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.279553Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"639.635µs","elapsed_secs":0.000639635},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:24:49.383229141Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:24:49.383104Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"902.101µs","elapsed_secs":0.000902101},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:19.273779582Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.273694Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.019733ms","elapsed_secs":0.001019733},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.27501338Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.274942Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"758.987µs","elapsed_secs":0.000758987},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.275318227Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.275257Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"848.639µs","elapsed_secs":0.000848639},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.276381321Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.276301Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"687.816µs","elapsed_secs":0.000687816},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.27674416Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.276654Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"798.758µs","elapsed_secs":0.000798758},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.277720112Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.277633Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"624.384µs","elapsed_secs":0.000624384},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.278232394Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.278145Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"770.718µs","elapsed_secs":0.000770718},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.279654347Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.279572Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"775.078µs","elapsed_secs":0.000775078},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:19.332097453Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.331930Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"785.328µs","elapsed_secs":0.000785328},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:19.488719167Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:19.488642Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.041034ms","elapsed_secs":0.001041034},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:23.645048488Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:23.644921Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"673.486µs","elapsed_secs":0.000673486},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:23.649437789Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:23.649343Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"797.988µs","elapsed_secs":0.000797988},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:23.650957484Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:23.650857Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"801.398µs","elapsed_secs":0.000801398},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:38.64653533Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.646368Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        GROUP BY status\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"778.698µs","elapsed_secs":0.000778698},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.648000423Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.647833Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"749.358µs","elapsed_secs":0.000749358},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.650675275Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.650510Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"735.247µs","elapsed_secs":0.000735247},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.652079037Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.651985Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"758.008µs","elapsed_secs":0.000758008},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.653702285Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.653610Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"863.44µs","elapsed_secs":0.00086344},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.655165148Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.655077Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"751.127µs","elapsed_secs":0.000751127},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.656663113Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.656542Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"716.287µs","elapsed_secs":0.000716287},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:38.658076315Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:38.657954Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"688.685µs","elapsed_secs":0.000688685},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T16:25:49.275466608Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.275296Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.971686ms","elapsed_secs":0.001971686},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.275702153Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.275616Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status = 'scheduled' AND starts_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.227501ms","elapsed_secs":0.002227501},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.277200758Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.277113Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"801.118µs","elapsed_secs":0.000801118},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.27947029Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.279387Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"3.02173ms","elapsed_secs":0.00302173},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.279576792Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.279511Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.72313ms","elapsed_secs":0.00172313},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.280896103Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.280835Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"654.035µs","elapsed_secs":0.000654035},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.282810237Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.282717Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.175807ms","elapsed_secs":0.001175807},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.28337485Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.283280Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.837735ms","elapsed_secs":0.002837735},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.285016047Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.284846Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.086865ms","elapsed_secs":0.001086865},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:25:49.382812748Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:25:49.382675Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"901.861µs","elapsed_secs":0.000901861},"target":"sqlx::query"}
