2025-11-28T16:26:19.284583325Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.284020Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"811.749µs","elapsed_secs":0.000811749},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.284606055Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.284377Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.738423ms","elapsed_secs":0.002738423},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.287584344Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.286306Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.108835ms","elapsed_secs":0.001108835},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.334812891Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.334613Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"859.33µs","elapsed_secs":0.00085933},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.383327497Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.383193Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"921.081µs","elapsed_secs":0.000921081},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.383359988Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.383236Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"message":"[DeliveryMatchingWorker] Aucune livraison à traiter (batch = 10)","log.target":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker","log.module_path":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker","log.file":"src/tasks/delivery_matching_worker.rs","log.line":61},"target":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.487247168Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.487075Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT delivery_id, suggested_status, created_at, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            delivery_id,\n            suggested_status,\n            created_at,\n            auto_confirm_after_seconds\n        FROM delivery_proximity_suggestions\n        WHERE \n            status = 'pending'\n            AND auto_confirm_after_seconds IS NOT NULL\n            AND created_at + (auto_confirm_after_seconds || ' seconds')::interval <= NOW()\n        LIMIT 50\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"855.82µs","elapsed_secs":0.00085582},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T16:26:19.48905Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T16:26:19.488950Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.100455ms","elapsed_secs":0.001100455},"target":"sqlx::query"}
